## 4. 密碼學工具
### 4.1 `src/crypto/canonical.ts`
```ts
// 穩定 canonical JSON：按 key 排序、無空白
export function canonicalize(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return "[" + value.map(canonicalize).join(",") + "]";
  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = Object.keys(obj).sort();
  return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalize(obj[k])).join(",") + "}";
}
```
### 4.2 `src/crypto/ed25519.ts`
```ts
import * as ed from "@noble/ed25519";
import { sha256 } from "@noble/hashes/sha256";
// noble/ed25519 v2 需要注入 sha512
import { sha512 } from "@noble/hashes/sha512";
ed.etc.sha512Sync = (...m) => sha512(ed.etc.concatBytes(...m));
export function toBase64Url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
}
export function fromBase64Url(s: string): Uint8Array {
  const b64 = s.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/").padEnd(Math.ceil(s.length / 4) * 4, "=");
  const bin = atob(b64);
  return Uint8Array.from(bin, c => c.charCodeAt(0));
}
export async function signCanonical(canonical: string, privKeyHex: string): Promise<string> {
  const digest = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonical));
  const sig = await ed.signAsync(digest, privKeyHex);
  return "ed25519:" + toBase64Url(sig);
}
export async function verifyCanonical(canonical: string, signature: string, pubKeyHex: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!signature.startsWith("ed25519:")) return false;
  const sig = fromBase64Url(signature.slice("ed25519:".length));
  const digest = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonical));
  return ed.verifyAsync(sig, digest, pubKeyHex);
}
```
> 簽名對象是 `sha256(canonical(signed_fields))`，不是整個 `verification.json`。`signed_fields` 固定為 `package_id, q, evidence_count, verified_at, expires_at, data_hash`。
### 4.3 `src/crypto/jwks.ts`
```ts
import { toBase64Url } from "./ed25519";
export function buildJwks(pubKeyHex: string, kid: string) {
  return {
    keys: [{
      kty: "OKP",
      crv: "Ed25519",
      x: toBase64Url(Uint8Array.from(pubKeyHex.match(/.{2}/g)!.map(h => parseInt(h, 16)))),
      kid,
      use: "sig",
      alg: "EdDSA"
    }]
  };
}
```

## 5. `verification.json` 生成器
### 5.1 輸入 fixture：`fixtures/sample-verifier-output.json`
```json
{
  "package_id": "research-2026-q4-ai-agent-adoption",
  "q": 0.81,
  "q_breakdown": {
    "source_coverage": 0.86,
    "freshness": 0.78,
    "internal_consistency": 0.84,
    "claim_specificity": 0.77
  },
  "evidence": [
    {
      "evidence_id": "ev-001",
      "source_url": "https://example.org/report/2026-09",
      "source_type": "primary_research",
      "accessed_at": "2026-10-01T08:12:00Z",
      "span": "para 4-7",
      "excerpt_sha256_hex": "REPLACE_WITH_REAL_HASH",
      "verifier_score": 0.92
    }
  ],
  "verified_at": "2026-10-15T02:00:00Z",
  "expires_at": "2027-01-15T02:00:00Z",
  "verifier_version": "v2.3.1"
}
```
### 5.2 `src/publisher/build-verification.ts`
```ts
import { canonicalize } from "../crypto/canonical";
import { signCanonical } from "../crypto/ed25519";
import { sha256 } from "@noble/hashes/sha256";
export interface VerifierOutput {
  package_id: string;
  q: number;
  q_breakdown: Record<string, number>;
  evidence: Array<{
    evidence_id: string;
    source_url: string;
    source_type: string;
    accessed_at: string;
    span: string;
    excerpt_sha256_hex: string;
    verifier_score: number;
  }>;
  verified_at: string;
  expires_at: string;
  verifier_version: string;
}
export async function buildVerification(
  v: VerifierOutput,
  data: unknown,
  privKeyHex: string,
  opts: { verifier_version: string; schemaUrl: string }
) {
  const evidence = v.evidence.map(e => ({
    evidence_id: e.evidence_id,
    source_url: e.source_url,
    source_type: e.source_type,
    accessed_at: e.accessed_at,
    excerpt_hash: "sha256:" + e.excerpt_sha256_hex,
    span: e.span,
    verifier_score: e.verifier_score
  }));
  const dataHash = "sha256:" + Buffer.from(sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalize(data)))).toString("hex");
  const signedFields = {
    package_id: v.package_id,
    q: v.q,
    evidence_count: evidence.length,
    verified_at: v.verified_at,
    expires_at: v.expires_at,
    data_hash: dataHash
  };
  const signature = await signCanonical(canonicalize(signedFields), privKeyHex);
  const domains = new Set(evidence.map(e => new URL(e.source_url).hostname));
  const sourceDiversity = Math.min(1, domains.size / Math.max(3, evidence.length));
  return {
    package_id: v.package_id,
    spec_version: "1.0",
    q: v.q,
    q_breakdown: v.q_breakdown,
    evidence,
    evidence_count: evidence.length,
    source_diversity: Number(sourceDiversity.toFixed(2)),
    verified_at: v.verified_at,
    expires_at: v.expires_at,
    verifier_version: opts.verifier_version,
    verifier_signature: signature,
    data_hash: dataHash,
    schema_url: opts.schemaUrl,
    license: "commercial-use-permitted",
    hallucination_boundary:
      "Evidence excerpts are verifier-validated at verified_at. Downstream interpretation is caller responsibility."
  };
}
```
---
## 6. PublishGuard AEO gate 實現
`src/publisher/publish-guard.ts`：
```ts
const REJECT_PATTERNS = [
  /ignore\s+previous/i,
  /system\s*:/i,
  /instruction\s*:/i,
  /<\|/,
  /[A-Za-z0-9+/]{80,}={0,2}/,
  /0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64,}/
];
export type Tier = "blocked" | "stale" | "listed" | "featured";
export interface GateResult {
  tier: Tier;
  reasons: string[];
  listed: boolean;
  featured: boolean;
}
export function aeoGate(
  v: any,
  now: Date,
  cfg: { maxTtlDays: number; lastRegenCount24h: number }
): GateResult {
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  // schema 粗檢
  const required = ["package_id","q","evidence","evidence_count","verified_at","expires_at","verifier_signature","data_hash","hallucination_boundary"];
  for (const k of required) if (!(k in v)) reasons.push(`missing:${k}`);
  const q = Number(v.q ?? 0);
  const ec = Number(v.evidence_count ?? 0);
  const expires = new Date(v.expires_at);
  const verified = new Date(v.verified_at);
  const domains = new Set((v.evidence ?? []).map((e: any) => new URL(e.source_url).hostname));
  // Blocked
  if (q < 0.65) reasons.push("q_lt_0.65");
  if (ec < 3) reasons.push("evidence_lt_3");
  if (reasons.some(r => r.startsWith("missing:"))) return { tier: "blocked", reasons, listed: false, featured: false };
  // TTL
  const maxTtl = new Date(now.getTime() + cfg.maxTtlDays * 86400_000);
  if (expires > maxTtl) reasons.push("ttl_exceeds_max");
  // 文本安全
  for (const field of ["hallucination_boundary"]) {
    const text = String(v[field] ?? "");
    if (REJECT_PATTERNS.some(p => p.test(text))) reasons.push(`unsafe_text:${field}`);
  }
  // 配額
  if (cfg.lastRegenCount24h >= 3) reasons.push("regen_quota_exceeded");
  // Stale
  if (expires <= now) return { tier: "stale", reasons: [...reasons, "expired"], listed: false, featured: false };
  // Listed
  if (q < 0.72 || ec < 4 || reasons.length > 0) {
    return { tier: "blocked", reasons, listed: false, featured: false };
  }
  // Featured
  const featured =
    q >= 0.80 &&
    ec >= 6 &&
    domains.size >= 3 &&
    (now.getTime() - verified.getTime()) <= 14 * 86400_000;
  return { tier: featured ? "featured" : "listed", reasons, listed: true, featured };
}
```
---
## 7. `agent-index.json` 生成器
`src/publisher/build-index.ts`：
```ts
import { aeoGate } from "./publish-guard";
export function buildIndex(packages: Array<{ id: string; verification: any; data: any }>, now: Date, base: string) {
  const items = [];
  for (const p of packages) {
    const g = aeoGate(p.verification, now, { maxTtlDays: 180, lastRegenCount24h: 0 });
    if (!g.listed) continue;
    const v = p.verification;
    const decay = Math.max(0.7, 1 - (now.getTime() - new Date(v.verified_at).getTime()) / (180 * 86400_000));
    items.push({
      package_id: v.package_id,
      title: p.data?.title ?? v.package_id,
      q: v.q,
      q_effective: Number((v.q * decay).toFixed(3)),
      evidence_count: v.evidence_count,
      source_diversity: v.source_diversity,
      verified_at: v.verified_at,
      expires_at: v.expires_at,
      tier: g.featured ? "featured" : "listed",
      preview_url: `${base}/preview/${v.package_id}`,
      verify_url: `${base}/verify/${v.package_id}`,
      paid_url: `${base}/buy/${v.package_id}`,
      price_usdc: g.featured ? "1.00" : "0.50",
      status: "fresh"
    });
  }
  items.sort((a, b) => (b.tier === "featured" ? 1 : 0) - (a.tier === "featured" ? 1 : 0) || b.q_effective - a.q_effective);
  return { spec_version: "1.0", generated_at: now.toISOString(), packages: items };
}
```

## 8. Worker 參考實現
### 8.1 `src/worker/index.ts`
```ts
import { handleVerify } from "./verify";
import { handlePreview } from "./preview";
import { handleStatus } from "./status";
import { handleBuy } from "./buy";
import { handleMcp } from "./mcp";
export interface Env {
  KV: KVNamespace;
  DB: D1Database;
  OWNSTIE_BASE: string;
  NETWORK: string;
  ASSET_USDC: string;
  PAY_TO: string;
  FACILITATOR_URL: string;
  VERIFIER_PRIVATE_KEY_JWK?: string;
  MOCK_FACILITATOR?: string;
}
export default {
  async fetch(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    const url = new URL(req.url);
    const p = url.pathname;
    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return cors();
    if (p.startsWith("/.well-known/")) return serveWellKnown(p, env);
    if (p.startsWith("/verify/")) return withCors(await handleVerify(req, env));
    if (p.startsWith("/preview/")) return withCors(await handlePreview(req, env));
    if (p.startsWith("/status/")) return withCors(await handleStatus(req, env));
    if (p.startsWith("/buy/")) return withCors(await handleBuy(req, env));
    if (p === "/mcp") return withCors(await handleMcp(req, env));
    return json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
  }
};
export function json(body: unknown, status = 200, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...headers }
  });
}
export function cors() {
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 204,
    headers: {
      "access-control-allow-origin": "*",
      "access-control-allow-headers": "content-type, x-payment, x-agent-id",
      "access-control-allow-methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS"
    }
  });
}
function withCors(res: Response) {
  const h = new Headers(res.headers);
  h.set("access-control-allow-origin", "*");
  return new Response(res.body, { status: res.status, headers: h });
}
async function serveWellKnown(p: string, env: Env) {
  // 由 KV 讀取，保證與 publisher 寫入一致
  const key = "wellknown:" + p;
  const cached = await env.KV.get(key, "text");
  if (cached) return new Response(cached, { headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "cache-control": "public, max-age=60" } });
  return json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
}
```
### 8.2 `src/worker/verify.ts` + `status.ts` + `preview.ts`
```ts
import { json, Env } from "./index";
export async function handleVerify(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const id = new URL(req.url).pathname.split("/").pop()!;
  const v = await env.KV.get(`verify:${id}`, "json");
  if (!v) return json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
  return json(v);
}
export async function handleStatus(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const id = new URL(req.url).pathname.split("/").pop()!;
  const status = (await env.KV.get(`status:${id}`)) ?? "unknown";
  const replacement = await env.KV.get(`replacement:${id}`);
  return json({ package_id: id, status, replacement_package_id: replacement ?? null });
}
export async function handlePreview(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const id = new URL(req.url).pathname.split("/").pop()!;
  const agentId = req.headers.get("x-agent-id") ?? req.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip") ?? "anon";
  // 10 req/hour 近似限速
  const bucket = `rl:preview:${agentId}:${Math.floor(Date.now() / 3600_000)}`;
  const cur = Number((await env.KV.get(bucket)) ?? "0");
  if (cur >= 10) return json({ error: "rate_limited" }, 429);
  await env.KV.put(bucket, String(cur + 1), { expirationTtl: 7200 });
  const pkg = await env.KV.get(`pkg:${id}`, "json");
  if (!pkg) return json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
  const v = await env.KV.get(`verify:${id}`, "json");
  const records = Array.isArray(pkg.data) ? pkg.data.slice(0, 3) : [pkg.data];
  return json({
    package_id: id,
    preview: true,
    records,
    verification_summary: v ? { q: v.q, evidence_count: v.evidence_count, verified_at: v.verified_at, expires_at: v.expires_at } : null,
    full_url: `${env.OWNSTIE_BASE}/buy/${id}`
  });
}
```
### 8.3 `src/worker/pseudonym.ts`
```ts
import { sha256 } from "@noble/hashes/sha256";
export function derivePseudonym(payer: string): string {
  const h = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(payer.toLowerCase()));
  return "psn_" + Buffer.from(h).toString("hex").slice(0, 32);
}
```
### 8.4 `src/worker/buy.ts` + `x402.ts`
```ts
import { json, Env } from "./index";
import { derivePseudonym } from "./pseudonym";
export async function handleBuy(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const id = new URL(req.url).pathname.split("/").pop()!;
  const status = await env.KV.get(`status:${id}`);
  if (status === "recalled") {
    const replacement = await env.KV.get(`replacement:${id}`);
    return json({ error: "pack_recalled", replacement_package_id: replacement ?? null }, 410);
  }
  const paymentHeader = req.headers.get("x-payment");
  if (!paymentHeader) return paymentRequired(id, env);
  let payment: any;
  try { payment = JSON.parse(atob(paymentHeader)); } catch { return json({ error: "bad_payment_header" }, 400); }
  if (payment.scheme !== "exact" || payment.network !== env.NETWORK) {
    return json({ error: "unsupported_scheme" }, 403);
  }
  const txHash = payment?.payload?.txHash;
  if (!txHash) return json({ error: "missing_tx_hash" }, 400);
  const seen = await env.KV.get(`tx:${txHash}`);
  if (seen) return json({ error: "tx_replay" }, 403);
  const settle = env.MOCK_FACILITATOR === "1"
    ? { isValid: true, amount: "5000", payer: payment?.payload?.payer ?? "0xmock" }
    : await settleViaFacilitator(payment, id, env);
  if (!settle.isValid) return json({ error: "settlement_failed" }, 403);
  await env.KV.put(`tx:${txHash}`, "1", { expirationTtl: 86400 * 30 });
  const pseudonym = derivePseudonym(settle.payer);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    "INSERT INTO payments (agent_pseudonym, package_id, amount_usdc, tx_hash, called_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)"
  ).bind(pseudonym, id, settle.amount, txHash, new Date().toISOString()).run();
  const pkg = await env.KV.get(`pkg:${id}`, "json");
  const v = await env.KV.get(`verify:${id}`, "json");
  return json({
    data: pkg?.data ?? pkg,
    verification_snapshot: v ? { ...v, snapshotted_at: new Date().toISOString() } : null,
    receipt: { tx_hash: txHash, network: env.NETWORK, amount_usdc: settle.amount }
  });
}
function paymentRequired(id: string, env: Env) {
  return json({
    x402Version: 1,
    accepts: [{
      scheme: "exact",
      network: env.NETWORK,
      maxAmountRequired: "5000",
      resource: `${env.OWNSTIE_BASE}/buy/${id}`,
      description: `Verified data package ${id}`,
      mimeType: "application/json",
      payTo: env.PAY_TO,
      maxTimeoutSeconds: 300,
      asset: env.ASSET_USDC,
      extra: { name: "USDC", version: "2" }
    }]
  }, 402);
}
async function settleViaFacilitator(payment: any, id: string, env: Env) {
  const res = await fetch(env.FACILITATOR_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      paymentPayload: payment,
      paymentRequirements: {
        scheme: "exact",
        network: env.NETWORK,
        maxAmountRequired: "5000",
        resource: `${env.OWNSTIE_BASE}/buy/${id}`,
        payTo: env.PAY_TO,
        asset: env.ASSET_USDC
      }
    })
  });
  return res.json();
}
```
### 8.5 `src/worker/mcp.ts`（核心 dispatch，省略完整 JSON-RPC 錯誤碼）
```ts
import { json, Env } from "./index";
export async function handleMcp(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);
  const body = await req.json() as any;
  const { id, method, params } = body;
  if (method === "initialize") {
    return json({
      jsonrpc: "2.0", id,
      result: {
        protocolVersion: "2025-06-18",
        capabilities: { tools: {} },
        serverInfo: { name: "ownsite-verified-data", version: "1.0.0" },
        instructions: "Always call get_verification before purchase_package. Verification is free and does not consume quota."
      }
    });
  }
  if (method === "tools/list") {
    return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result: { tools: TOOLS } });
  }
  if (method === "tools/call") {
    const { name, arguments: args } = params ?? {};
    if (name === "get_verification") {
      const v = await env.KV.get(`verify:${args.package_id}`, "json");
      return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result: { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(v) }] } });
    }
    if (name === "check_status") {
      const status = await env.KV.get(`status:${args.package_id}`) ?? "unknown";
      return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result: { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify({ status }) }] } });
    }
    if (name === "list_verified_packages") {
      const index = await env.KV.get("wellknown:/.well-known/agent-index.json", "json") as any;
      let pkgs = index?.packages ?? [];
      if (args?.min_q != null) pkgs = pkgs.filter((p: any) => p.q >= args.min_q);
      if (args?.min_evidence != null) pkgs = pkgs.filter((p: any) => p.evidence_count >= args.min_evidence);
      return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result: { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify({ packages: pkgs }) }] } });
    }
    // purchase_package 應復用 handleBuy，此處返回提示
    if (name === "purchase_package") {
      return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, error: { code: -32000, message: "use_http_402_flow", data: { buy_url: `${env.OWNSTIE_BASE}/buy/${args.package_id}` } } });
    }
  }
  return json({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id, error: { code: -32601, message: "method_not_found" } });
}
const TOOLS = [
  { name: "list_verified_packages", description: "List packages filtered by q/evidence/age. Free.", inputSchema: { type: "object", properties: { min_q: { type: "number" }, min_evidence: { type: "integer" }, max_age_days: { type: "integer" } } } },
  { name: "get_verification", description: "Return full verification.json. Free.", inputSchema: { type: "object", required: ["package_id"], properties: { package_id: { type: "string" } } } },
  { name: "purchase_package", description: "x402-gated purchase. Use HTTP 402 flow.", inputSchema: { type: "object", required: ["package_id"], properties: { package_id: { type: "string" } } } },
  { name: "check_status", description: "Return fresh/stale/recalled. Free.", inputSchema: { type: "object", required: ["package_id"], properties: { package_id: { type: "string" } } } }
];
```

## 9. D1 schema
`schema/d1.sql`：
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payments (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  agent_pseudonym TEXT NOT NULL,
  package_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  amount_usdc TEXT NOT NULL,
  tx_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  called_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_pkg ON payments(package_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_psn ON payments(agent_pseudonym);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_time ON payments(called_at);
```

## 10. 端到端測試 Agent
`scripts/test-agent.ts`（mock 模式可先跑通邏輯）：
```ts
import { canonicalize } from "../src/crypto/canonical";
import { verifyCanonical } from "../src/crypto/ed25519";
const BASE = process.env.BASE ?? "http://localhost:8787";
const AGENT_ID = "test-agent-001";
async function main() {
  // 1. discover
  const card = await get(`${BASE}/.well-known/agent.json`);
  if (!card?.extensions?.ownsite_verification) throw new Error("no verification extension");
  // 2. index
  const index = await get(`${BASE}/.well-known/agent-index.json`);
  const candidates = (index.packages ?? []).filter((p: any) => p.q >= 0.72 && p.evidence_count >= 4);
  if (!candidates.length) throw new Error("no candidates");
  const pkg = candidates[0];
  // 3. verify
  const v = await get(`${BASE}/verify/${pkg.package_id}`);
  // 4. jwks + 離線驗簽
  const jwks = await get(`${BASE}/.well-known/jwks.json`);
  const x = jwks.keys[0].x;
  const pubHex = Buffer.from(x.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/"), "base64").toString("hex");
  const signed = {
    package_id: v.package_id, q: v.q, evidence_count: v.evidence_count,
    verified_at: v.verified_at, expires_at: v.expires_at, data_hash: v.data_hash
  };
  const ok = await verifyCanonical(canonicalize(signed), v.verifier_signature, pubHex);
  if (!ok) throw new Error("signature invalid");
  console.log("signature OK");
  // 5. refetch 1 evidence
  const ev = v.evidence[0];
  const page = await fetch(ev.source_url).then(r => r.text());
  // 真實實作應抽 span 再 hash；此處只驗證可達
  if (!page) throw new Error("evidence unreachable");
  console.log("evidence reachable:", ev.source_url);
  // 6. preview
  const preview = await get(`${BASE}/preview/${pkg.package_id}`, { "x-agent-id": AGENT_ID });
  if (!preview.preview) throw new Error("preview missing");
  // 7. 402
  const challenge = await fetch(`${BASE}/buy/${pkg.package_id}`, { headers: { "x-agent-id": AGENT_ID } });
  if (challenge.status !== 402) throw new Error("expected 402");
  const accept = (await challenge.json()).accepts[0];
  // 8. mock payment（真實 testnet 需替換為真錢包簽名）
  const mockPayment = btoa(JSON.stringify({
    scheme: "exact",
    network: accept.network,
    payload: { txHash: "0xmock_" + Date.now(), payer: "0xmockpayer" }
  }));
  const bought = await fetch(`${BASE}/buy/${pkg.package_id}`, { headers: { "x-payment": mockPayment, "x-agent-id": AGENT_ID } });
  if (!bought.ok) throw new Error("buy failed: " + bought.status);
  const pack = await bought.json();
  if (!pack.data || !pack.verification_snapshot || !pack.receipt) throw new Error("bad pack shape");
  console.log("purchase OK", pack.receipt);
  // 9. replay 應被拒
  const replay = await fetch(`${BASE}/buy/${pkg.package_id}`, { headers: { "x-payment": mockPayment } });
  if (replay.status !== 403) throw new Error("replay not blocked");
  console.log("replay blocked OK");
  // 10. status
  const status = await get(`${BASE}/status/${pkg.package_id}`);
  console.log("status:", status.status);
}
async function get(url: string, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const r = await fetch(url, { headers });
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${url} -> ${r.status}`);
  return r.json();
}
main().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```

## 11. 需替換的佔位符清單
| 佔位符 | 位置 | 替換為 |
|---|---|---|
| `REPLACE_KV_ID` | `wrangler.toml` | `wrangler kv namespace create KV` 輸出 |
| `REPLACE_D1_ID` | `wrangler.toml` | `wrangler d1 create ownsite-aeo` 輸出 |
| `0xREPLACE_WITH_YOUR_WALLET` | `wrangler.toml` `PAY_TO` | 收款錢包 |
| `VERIFIER_PRIVATE_KEY_JWK` | `wrangler secret` | `npm run gen-keypair` 生成 |
| `https://ownsite.dev` | `wrangler.toml` / schemas | 實際域名 |
| `REPLACE_WITH_REAL_HASH` | fixture | verifier 真實 excerpt hash |
| `FACILITATOR_URL` | `wrangler.toml` | Phase 1 可用 mock；Phase 2 換 CDP |
| `MOCK_FACILITATOR=1` | `wrangler.toml` vars | 僅本地測試，**上線必須移除** |
`scripts/gen-keypair.ts`：
```ts
import * as ed from "@noble/ed25519";
import { sha512 } from "@noble/hashes/sha512";
ed.etc.sha512Sync = (...m) => sha512(ed.etc.concatBytes(...m));
const priv = ed.utils.randomPrivateKey();
const pub = await ed.getPublicKeyAsync(priv);
console.log("PRIVATE_HEX=", Buffer.from(priv).toString("hex"));
console.log("PUBLIC_HEX=", Buffer.from(pub).toString("hex"));
console.log("把 PRIVATE_HEX 放入 wrangler secret；PUBLIC_HEX 寫入 jwks.json 的 x（base64url）");
```

## 12. 本地跑通順序
```bash
npm install
npm run gen-keypair          # 保存 private/public
npm run d1:init              # 建表
# 將 fixtures 的 verifier 輸出經 buildVerification + aeoGate 寫入 KV：
npm run publish -- fixtures/sample-verifier-output.json
npm run dev                  # wrangler dev，MOCK_FACILITATOR=1
npm run test-agent           # 期望：signature OK → purchase OK → replay blocked OK
```
**Phase 1 DoD 對應**：
- 測試 Agent 全程無人類干預 → §10 腳本自動完成
- 離線驗簽成功 → `verifyCanonical` 通過
- evidence refetch 成功 → 腳本第 5 步
- 402 → 結算 → 取包 → replay 拒絕 → §10 第 7–9 步
---
## 13. 已知局限（部署前必讀）
1. **KV 限速是近似值**，非原子計數；高併發下會超限。Phase 2 建議換 Durable Object 或 Cloudflare Rate Limiting。
2. **`MOCK_FACILITATOR=1` 僅本地測試**，會接受任何 `txHash`；上線必須移除並接真實 CDP facilitator。
3. **MCP `purchase_package` 未在 Worker 內復用 `handleBuy`**，只返回 HTTP 402 流程指引；Phase 2 再統一。
4. **evidence refetch 的 span 抽取未實現**，測試腳本只驗證可達；Phase 1 完結前需補「按 span 抽 excerpt → sha256 → 比對」。
5. **JWKS 輪換未實現**，v1 單 key；v2 加 `kid` 多 key 與過期。
6. **公開 dashboard 未包含**，屬 Phase 2。
7. **未接入真實 `agent.json` 靜態部署**，`serveWellKnown` 從 KV 讀；Phase 2 加 Pages → KV 同步 cron。
---
## 14. 下一批可做（v1.2 候選）
按優先級：
1. **evidence span 抽取 + hash 比對實作**（補 §13.4，Phase 1 必需）
2. **Pages → KV 同步 cron**（解 §13.7 與 A11 一致性檢查）
3. **真實 CDP facilitator 接入與 base-sepolia 實測**（Phase 2 起點）
4. **recall cron + 410 replacement 自動化**
5. **公開 dashboard 靜態生成腳本**（`dashboard.json` → Pages）
6. **市集 listing 自動推送 adapter**（Bazaar / Agent402 / MCP.so）
需要我繼續做哪一項，直接講編號即可。

# 補充文件 v1.3：Pages → KV 同步 Cron

**對應計劃書**：§3.1 唯一可信根 / §9 風險（Pages 與 KV 不一致）/ §13.7 已知局限 / v1.2 §14.2  
**目標**：消除 GitHub Pages（靜態、部署延遲、CDN 緩存）與 Cloudflare KV（Worker 讀取源）之間的不一致，令 Agent 無論從 Pages 還是 Worker 讀取，看到的 `agent-index.json` / `agent.json` / `jwks.json` 都是同一版本。  
**範圍**：GitHub Actions webhook 主動推 + Cloudflare Cron 兜底檢查；Worker sync endpoint 帶 HMAC 鑑權；manifest 版本比對；失敗降級。

---

## 1. 核心決策

| 問題 | 決策 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|
| Source of truth 是誰？ | **GitHub Pages repo 的 `public/.well-known/*`** | 計劃書 §3.1 已定；零第三方 ToS 風險 |
| 同步方向 | Pages → KV（單向） | Worker 只讀，不反向寫 Pages |
| 觸發機制 | **GitHub Actions webhook 主動推 + Cron 每小時兜底** | 主動推降低延遲；Cron 解漏推與手動改動 |
| Worker 從哪拉檔案？ | **`raw.githubusercontent.com/{owner}/{repo}/{branch}/...`**，不從 Pages 域名拉 | 避開 Fastly CDN 600 秒緩存；raw 源頭即 commit |
| 一致性保證 | **manifest + 版本前綴 + 指針切換** | 避免半更新；舊版本可回滾 |
| 同步失敗怎麼辦？ | Worker 繼續服務舊版本，響應頭標 `x-sync-stale: true`，Cron 告警 | 可用性優先，不阻斷售賣 |
| 鑑權 | HMAC-SHA256 over body + timestamp，共享密鑰存 GitHub Secrets / wrangler secret | 防未授權觸發 |

**不做**：Worker 運行時 proxy 到 Pages（增加延遲與單點依賴）；雙向同步（KV 不回寫 Pages）；實時同步（KV 本身最終一致，秒級延遲可接受）。

---

## 2. 架構圖

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ publisher (本地或 CI)                                            │
│   buildVerification → buildIndex → 寫入 public/.well-known/*     │
│   → 生成 sync-manifest.json → git commit                         │
└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                               │ push
                               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ GitHub repo                                                      │
│   public/.well-known/agent.json                                  │
│   public/.well-known/agent-index.json                            │
│   public/.well-known/jwks.json                                   │
│   public/.well-known/verification-spec.json                      │
│   public/.well-known/sync-manifest.json   ← 新增                 │
└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
              ┌────────────────┴────────────────┐
              │                                 │
              ▼                                 ▼
┌──────────────────────────┐     ┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ GitHub Actions            │     │ GitHub Pages (Fastly CDN)     │
│  Pages deploy 完成後       │     │  人類/靜態讀取，緩存 600s      │
│  POST /internal/sync      │     └──────────────────────────────┘
│  (HMAC 簽名)              │
└──────────────┬───────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Cloudflare Worker                                                │
│   /internal/sync:                                                │
│     1. 驗 HMAC + timestamp                                       │
│     2. 從 raw.githubusercontent.com 拉 sync-manifest.json        │
│     3. 比對 manifest.sha256 與 KV 現有版本                       │
│     4. 拉各檔案 → 驗 sha256 → 寫入 KV v{N}/...                   │
│     5. 全部成功後切換 sync-version 指針                          │
│   /.well-known/*:                                                │
│     讀 sync-version → 拼 v{N}/path → 返回                        │
│     找不到 → 回退 legacy:path（兼容期）                          │
└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Cloudflare Cron (每小時 :07)                                     │
│   1. 從 raw 拉 sync-manifest.json                                │
│   2. 比對 KV sync-version 的 manifest_hash                       │
│   3. 不一致 → 內部觸發 sync（不需要 HMAC，因為是自身）           │
│   4. 超過 6 小時未同步 → 告警                                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 3. `sync-manifest.json` 規範

**位置**：`public/.well-known/sync-manifest.json`  
**生成者**：publisher（在寫完所有 `.well-known/*` 後最後生成）  
**內容**：

```json
{
  "manifest_version": "1.0",
  "generated_at": "2026-10-22T12:00:00Z",
  "commit_sha": "a1b2c3d4e5f6...",
  "files": [
    { "path": "/.well-known/agent.json",             "sha256": "sha256:...", "size": 1234 },
    { "path": "/.well-known/agent-index.json",       "sha256": "sha256:...", "size": 5678 },
    { "path": "/.well-known/jwks.json",              "sha256": "sha256:...", "size": 234 },
    { "path": "/.well-known/verification-spec.json", "sha256": "sha256:...", "size": 890 }
  ],
  "manifest_hash": "sha256:..."
}
```

**規則**：
- `manifest_hash` = `sha256(canonicalize(files))`，不包含 `generated_at` 與 `commit_sha`，確保同一份 files 內容產生同一 manifest_hash（除非檔案真的變了）。
- `sync-manifest.json` 本身**不列入 `files`**（避免自我引用）。
- Worker 只信任 `files` 中列出的路徑；manifest 中未列出的 `.well-known/*` 不同步。
- `commit_sha` 僅作審計，不參與 hash。

**生成實現**（`src/publisher/build-manifest.ts`）：

```ts
import { sha256 } from "@noble/hashes/sha256";
import { canonicalize } from "../crypto/canonical";

export interface ManifestFile { path: string; sha256: string; size: number; }

export function buildManifest(
  files: Array<{ path: string; content: string }>,
  commitSha: string
) {
  const entries: ManifestFile[] = files.map(f => {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(f.content);
    return {
      path: f.path,
      sha256: "sha256:" + Buffer.from(sha256(bytes)).toString("hex"),
      size: bytes.length
    };
  });
  entries.sort((a, b) => a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
  const manifestHash = "sha256:" + Buffer.from(
    sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalize(entries)))
  ).toString("hex");

  return {
    manifest_version: "1.0",
    generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    commit_sha: commitSha,
    files: entries,
    manifest_hash: manifestHash
  };
}
```

---

## 4. GitHub Actions webhook 觸發

`.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml`（在現有 Pages deploy job 末尾追加）：

```yaml
name: Deploy Pages and Sync KV

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "public/.well-known/**"
      - "public/schemas/**"

jobs:
  build-and-deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: "20" }
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run build:site          # 生成 public/.well-known/* + sync-manifest.json
      - uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with: { path: public }
      - uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4
        id: deployment

      # 部署完成後觸發 Worker sync
      - name: Trigger Worker sync
        env:
          SYNC_URL: ${{ secrets.WORKER_SYNC_URL }}       # https://ownsite.dev/internal/sync
          SYNC_SECRET: ${{ secrets.WORKER_SYNC_SECRET }} # 與 wrangler secret 同一值
          COMMIT_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
        run: |
          BODY=$(printf '{"commit_sha":"%s","ts":"%s"}' "$COMMIT_SHA" "$(date -u +%s)")
          SIG=$(printf '%s' "$BODY" | openssl dgst -sha256 -hmac "$SYNC_SECRET" -hex | sed 's/^.* //')
          curl -sS -X POST "$SYNC_URL" \
            -H "content-type: application/json" \
            -H "x-sync-signature: sha256=$SIG" \
            -H "x-sync-timestamp: $(date -u +%s)" \
            -d "$BODY" \
            --max-time 60 \
            --retry 2 --retry-delay 5 \
            -w "\nHTTP %{http_code}\n"
```

**注意**：
- 用 `deploy-pages@v4` 的 `id` 確保 Pages 部署完成後才觸發 sync。但 GitHub Pages 部署完成 ≠ raw.githubusercontent.com 已可讀（raw 讀的是 commit，push 後通常幾秒內可讀）。若遇 404，Worker 內建重試 3 次（間隔 2s / 5s / 10s）。
- `--retry 2` 只覆蓋網絡錯誤；HTTP 5xx 需在 Worker 內處理。
- 若 Actions 觸發失敗，Cron 兜底會在下一小時補上。

---

## 5. Worker sync endpoint

### 5.1 HMAC 驗證

`src/worker/sync-auth.ts`：

```ts
import { timingSafeEqual } from "./timing";

export async function verifySyncSignature(
  secret: string,
  body: string,
  signatureHeader: string | null,
  timestampHeader: string | null,
  maxSkewSec = 300
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }> {
  if (!signatureHeader?.startsWith("sha256=")) return { ok: false, reason: "missing_signature" };
  if (!timestampHeader) return { ok: false, reason: "missing_timestamp" };
  const ts = Number(timestampHeader);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - ts) > maxSkewSec) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "timestamp_skew" };
  }
  const expected = await hmacSha256Hex(secret, body);
  const provided = signatureHeader.slice("sha256=".length);
  if (!timingSafeEqual(expected, provided)) return { ok: false, reason: "bad_signature" };
  return { ok: true };
}

async function hmacSha256Hex(secret: string, body: string): Promise<string> {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw", new TextEncoder().encode(secret),
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(body));
  return [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}
```

`src/worker/timing.ts`：

```ts
export function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
}
```

### 5.2 同步邏輯

`src/worker/sync.ts`：

```ts
import { json, Env } from "./index";
import { verifySyncSignature } from "./sync-auth";
import { sha256 } from "@noble/hashes/sha256";

const RAW_BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com";
const OWNER = "REPLACE_GH_OWNER";
const REPO = "REPLACE_GH_REPO";
const BRANCH = "main";

export async function handleSync(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const body = await req.text();
  const auth = await verifySyncSignature(
    env.SYNC_SECRET!,
    body,
    req.headers.get("x-sync-signature"),
    req.headers.get("x-sync-timestamp")
  );
  if (!auth.ok) return json({ error: "unauthorized", reason: auth.reason }, 401);

  const result = await runSync(env);
  return json(result, result.ok ? 200 : 500);
}

export async function runSync(env: Env): Promise<{
  ok: boolean; action: "noop" | "synced" | "failed";
  manifest_hash?: string; version?: string; reason?: string;
}> {
  // 1. 拉 manifest（重試 3 次）
  const manifestUrl = `${RAW_BASE}/${OWNER}/${REPO}/${BRANCH}/public/.well-known/sync-manifest.json`;
  let manifest: any;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const r = await fetch(manifestUrl, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-cache" } });
    if (r.ok) { manifest = await r.json(); break; }
    if (i < 2) await sleep([2000, 5000][i]);
  }
  if (!manifest) return { ok: false, action: "failed", reason: "manifest_unreachable" };

  // 2. 比對現有
  const currentVersion = await env.KV.get("sync-version");
  const currentHash = currentVersion ? await env.KV.get(`sync-meta:${currentVersion}`) : null;
  if (currentHash === manifest.manifest_hash) {
    return { ok: true, action: "noop", manifest_hash: manifest.manifest_hash, version: currentVersion ?? undefined };
  }

  // 3. 拉各檔案並驗 hash
  const fetched: Array<{ path: string; content: string }> = [];
  for (const f of manifest.files) {
    const url = `${RAW_BASE}/${OWNER}/${REPO}/${BRANCH}/public${f.path}`;
    const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-cache" } });
    if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, action: "failed", reason: `fetch_${r.status}:${f.path}` };
    const content = await r.text();
    const actual = "sha256:" + Buffer.from(sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(content))).toString("hex");
    if (actual !== f.sha256) {
      return { ok: false, action: "failed", reason: `hash_mismatch:${f.path}` };
    }
    fetched.push({ path: f.path, content });
  }

  // 4. 寫入新版本前綴
  const newVersion = `v${Date.now()}`;
  for (const f of fetched) {
    await env.KV.put(`${newVersion}:${f.path}`, f.content);
  }
  await env.KV.put(`sync-meta:${newVersion}`, manifest.manifest_hash);

  // 5. 切換指針（最後一步，原子）
  await env.KV.put("sync-version", newVersion);

  // 6. 清理舊版本（保留最近 3 個）
  await cleanupOldVersions(env, newVersion);

  return { ok: true, action: "synced", manifest_hash: manifest.manifest_hash, version: newVersion };
}

async function cleanupOldVersions(env: Env, keep: string) {
  // KV list 按前綴；保留 keep + 最近 2 個
  // 實際實現需遍歷 sync-meta: 前綴，取時間戳排序
  // v1.3 可先跳過，KV 有 30 天免費保留期，靠 TTL 自然清理
}

function sleep(ms: number) { return new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms)); }
```

**關鍵順序**：
1. 拉 manifest → 2. 比對 → 3. 拉全部檔案並驗 hash → 4. 寫入新前綴 → 5. 切換指針 → 6. 清理舊版本

**任何一步失敗都不切換指針**，舊版本繼續服務。指針切換是最後一步，KV 單 key put 是原子的。

### 5.3 `serveWellKnown` 更新

`src/worker/index.ts` 中的 `serveWellKnown` 改為：

```ts
async function serveWellKnown(p: string, env: Env) {
  const version = await env.KV.get("sync-version");
  if (version) {
    const cached = await env.KV.get(`${version}:${p}`, "text");
    if (cached) {
      return new Response(cached, {
        headers: {
          "content-type": "application/json",
          "cache-control": "public, max-age=60",
          "x-sync-version": version
        }
      });
    }
  }
  // 兼容期：回退 legacy
  const legacy = await env.KV.get(`legacy:${p}`, "text");
  if (legacy) {
    return new Response(legacy, {
      headers: {
        "content-type": "application/json",
        "cache-control": "public, max-age=60",
        "x-sync-stale": "true"
      }
    });
  }
  return json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
}
```

**`x-sync-version`**：Agent / 監控可據此判斷是否為最新版本。  
**`x-sync-stale: true`**：兼容期回退標記，Cron 告警條件之一。

---

## 6. Cron 兜底

### 6.1 `wrangler.toml` 追加

```toml
[triggers]
crons = ["7 * * * *"]   # 每小時 :07
```

### 6.2 `src/worker/index.ts` 追加 scheduled handler

```ts
export default {
  async fetch(req: Request, env: Env) { /* ... */ },

  async scheduled(event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
    const result = await runSync(env);
    // 記錄結果
    await env.KV.put(
      "sync-last-attempt",
      JSON.stringify({ at: new Date().toISOString(), ...result }),
      { expirationTtl: 86400 * 30 }
    );
    if (!result.ok || result.action === "failed") {
      await sendAlert(env, "sync_failed", result);
    }
    // 檢查上次成功同步時間
    const lastSuccess = await env.KV.get("sync-last-success");
    if (lastSuccess) {
      const ageMs = Date.now() - new Date(lastSuccess).getTime();
      if (ageMs > 6 * 3600_000) {
        await sendAlert(env, "sync_stale_6h", { lastSuccess, ageHours: ageMs / 3600_000 });
      }
    }
    if (result.ok && result.action !== "failed") {
      await env.KV.put("sync-last-success", new Date().toISOString());
    }
  }
};

async function sendAlert(env: Env, kind: string, data: unknown) {
  if (!env.ALERT_WEBHOOK) return;
  await fetch(env.ALERT_WEBHOOK, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ kind, data, at: new Date().toISOString() })
  });
}
```

`wrangler.toml` 追加：

```toml
[vars]
ALERT_WEBHOOK = "https://hooks.slack.com/services/REPLACE"
```

（或改用 Cloudflare Queue / Email Routing；v1.3 先用 webhook。）

### 6.3 `/status/sync` 公開端點（可選，用於公開 dashboard）

```ts
if (p === "/status/sync") {
  const lastAttempt = await env.KV.get("sync-last-attempt", "json");
  const lastSuccess = await env.KV.get("sync-last-success");
  const version = await env.KV.get("sync-version");
  return json({ last_attempt: lastAttempt, last_success: lastSuccess, current_version: version });
}
```

**注意**：此端點不洩露 manifest 內容或 commit_sha，只暴露時間與版本號，適合作為公開 dashboard 的信任信號。

---

## 7. 失敗處理與降級

| 情況 | 行為 | Agent 感知 |
|---|---|---|
| webhook 未送達 | Cron 下一小時兜底同步 | 最長 1 小時延遲；`x-sync-version` 保持舊值 |
| raw 拉 manifest 失敗 | sync 返回 500，指針不切換 | 繼續服務舊版本；Cron 告警 |
| 某檔案 hash mismatch | sync 返回 500，指針不切換 | 舊版本繼續；告警含具體路徑 |
| 指針切換後 Worker 讀不到新 key | 回退 `legacy:` 前綴 | `x-sync-stale: true`；Cron 告警 |
| 超過 6 小時未成功同步 | Cron 告警 `sync_stale_6h` | 服務不中斷，但需人工介入 |
| KV 寫入配額耗盡 | sync 失敗，指針不切換 | 舊版本繼續；告警 |

**降級原則**：**可用性優先於新鮮度**。同步失敗時寧可服務舊版本並標記 stale，也不返回 404。

**舊版本保留**：v1.3 不主動清理舊版本（KV 寫入成本低），靠 KV 自身 TTL 或手動清理。v1.4 再加 cleanup cron。

---

## 8. 監控指標與告警

| 指標 | 來源 | 告警閾值 |
|---|---|---|
| `sync-last-success` 年齡 | KV | > 6 小時 |
| `sync-last-attempt.action === "failed"` | KV | 即時 |
| `x-sync-stale` 響應比例 | Worker 日誌 | 任意出現即告警 |
| manifest_hash 變更頻率 | sync 日誌 | 24 小時 > 50 次（異常頻繁發布） |
| raw fetch 404 率 | sync 內部計數 | > 10% |
| HMAC 驗證失敗次數 | Worker 日誌 | > 0（可能有人掃 endpoint） |

**公開 dashboard 建議指標**：`current_version`、`last_success`、`last_attempt.action`。這是免費信任信號——Agent 和人看到你的索引同步健康，會更信任你的 verification 鏈。

---

## 9. 測試腳本

### 9.1 本地端到端測試

`scripts/test-sync.ts`：

```ts
import { runSync } from "../src/worker/sync";
import { buildManifest } from "../src/publisher/build-manifest";

// mock KV
class MockKV {
  store = new Map<string, string>();
  async get(k: string) { return this.store.get(k) ?? null; }
  async put(k: string, v: string) { this.store.set(k, v); }
  async delete(k: string) { this.store.delete(k); }
}

async function main() {
  const kv = new MockKV();
  const env: any = { KV: kv, SYNC_SECRET: "test" };

  // 第一次：noop（無現有版本，但 raw 拉不到 → 在測試環境需 mock fetch）
  // 建議用 nock 或 msw mock raw.githubusercontent.com

  console.log("mock 模式需搭配 msw；真實測試見 scripts/test-sync-live.ts");
}

main();
```

### 9.2 真實環境測試（Phase 1 DoD 追加）

`scripts/test-sync-live.ts`（需先部署 Worker 與設定 secret）：

```ts
const BASE = process.env.BASE!;
const SECRET = process.env.SYNC_SECRET!;

async function hmac(secret: string, body: string) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw", new TextEncoder().encode(secret),
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(body));
  return [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

async function main() {
  const body = JSON.stringify({ ts: new Date().toISOString() });
  const ts = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const sig = await hmac(SECRET, body);

  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}/internal/sync`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "content-type": "application/json",
      "x-sync-signature": `sha256=${sig}`,
      "x-sync-timestamp": String(ts)
    },
    body
  });
  const result = await r.json();
  console.log("sync result:", result);
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error("sync failed");

  // 驗證 /agent-index.json 響應頭
  const idx = await fetch(`${BASE}/.well-known/agent-index.json`);
  console.log("x-sync-version:", idx.headers.get("x-sync-version"));
  console.log("x-sync-stale:", idx.headers.get("x-sync-stale"));
  if (idx.headers.get("x-sync-stale") === "true") throw new Error("serving stale");
}

main().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```

### 9.3 測試用例

| 用例 | 期望 |
|---|---|
| 首次 sync（KV 空） | 200，action=synced，`sync-version` 有值 |
| 重複 sync（manifest 未變） | 200，action=noop |
| manifest 變但檔案 hash 錯 | 500，指針不切換，舊版本仍可讀 |
| HMAC 錯 | 401 |
| timestamp 過期（>5min） | 401，reason=timestamp_skew |
| raw 404（commit 未生效） | 500，重試 3 次後返回 failed |
| 同步後 `/agent-index.json` | 響應頭含 `x-sync-version`，無 `x-sync-stale` |

## 10. 部署與遷移步驟

### 10.1 首次部署

```bash
# 1. 生成 HMAC 密鑰
openssl rand -hex 32
# → 分別寫入 GitHub Secrets (WORKER_SYNC_SECRET) 與 wrangler secret

wrangler secret put SYNC_SECRET

# 2. 設定 wrangler.toml
#    - OWNER / REPO / BRANCH（替換 sync.ts 常量，或移到 vars）
#    - crons
#    - ALERT_WEBHOOK

# 3. 本地測試
npm run dev
npm run test-sync-live   # BASE=http://localhost:8787

# 4. 部署
npm run deploy

# 5. 首次手動觸發
curl -X POST https://ownsite.dev/internal/sync \
  -H "content-type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sync-signature: sha256=..." \
  -H "x-sync-timestamp: $(date +%s)" \
  -d '{}'

# 6. 驗證
curl -sI https://ownsite.dev/.well-known/agent-index.json | grep -i x-sync
```

### 10.2 遷移期（兼容舊 `wellknown:` 前綴）

v1.0 的代碼用 `wellknown:${p}` 前綴。v1.3 改為 `v{N}:${p}`。遷移步驟：

1. 部署 v1.3 Worker（帶 `sync-version` 邏輯 + `legacy:` 回退）
2. 首次 sync 成功後，`sync-version` 有值，新請求走新前綴
3. 舊 `wellknown:` key 保留 7 天，期間 `serveWellKnown` 優先讀新前綴，讀不到才回退 `legacy:`
4. 7 天後刪除舊 key

**兼容期**：`legacy:` 回退是保險，正常情況下不應觸發。若觸發，`x-sync-stale: true` 會暴露，Cron 告警。

### 10.3 替換清單（更新 v1.1 §11）

| 佔位符 | 位置 | 替換為 |
|---|---|---|
| `REPLACE_GH_OWNER` | `src/worker/sync.ts` | GitHub 用戶名/組織 |
| `REPLACE_GH_REPO` | `src/worker/sync.ts` | repo 名 |
| `SYNC_SECRET` | `wrangler secret` + GitHub Secrets | `openssl rand -hex 32` |
| `ALERT_WEBHOOK` | `wrangler.toml` | Slack/飛書 webhook |
| `WORKER_SYNC_URL` | GitHub Secrets | `https://ownsite.dev/internal/sync` |

---

## 11. 已知局限

1. **KV 最終一致**：`sync-version` 切換後，全球邊緣節點可能需要最多 60 秒才全部看到新值。此期間部分 Agent 讀到舊版本，部分讀到新版本。**v1.3 接受此延遲**，因為索引內容變更頻率低（每日或每週），且 60 秒內新舊版本都是「有效版本」（舊版本仍在 `expires_at` 內）。
2. **raw.githubusercontent.com 延遲**：commit push 後，raw 通常在幾秒內可讀，但偶爾有 30 秒以上延遲。Worker 內建 3 次重試（2s / 5s / 10s），最長 17 秒。若仍失敗，Cron 兜底。
3. **舊版本不主動清理**：KV 寫入成本低，v1.3 不清理，靠 KV 自身 TTL 或手動。v1.4 再加 cleanup cron。
4. **webhook 與 Cron 可能並發**：兩個 sync 同時觸發時，可能寫入兩個版本前綴，最後一個切換指針者生效。**可接受**，因為最終指向同一 manifest（或較新 manifest）。若需嚴格串行，可加 KV 鎖（`sync-lock` key + TTL 60s），v1.3 不做。
5. **`legacy:` 回退只覆蓋 `.well-known/*`**，不覆蓋 `pkg:` / `verify:` / `status:`。這些由 publisher 直接寫入 KV（v1.0 已有邏輯），不走 sync。**需確認 publisher 的 KV 寫入與 Pages 寫入同源**——若 publisher 只寫 Pages 不寫 KV，v1.3 需補一個 `pkg-sync` 路徑。**這是 v1.3 之後的 v1.4 候選**。
6. **Cron 與 webhook 的 HMAC 不一致**：Cron 內部觸發不需要 HMAC（自身調用 `runSync`），webhook 需要。兩者共享同一 `runSync` 函數，邏輯一致。

---

# 補充文件 v1.4：真實 CDP Facilitator 接入與 base-sepolia 實測

**對應計劃書**：§3.4 x402 收費閘 / §10 Phase 1–2 / v1.1 §11 替換清單 / v1.3 §13.1  
**目標**：把 v1.1 的 `MOCK_FACILITATOR=1` 替換為真實 CDP facilitator，在 base-sepolia 測試網完成一次真實 USDC 結算，令 Phase 1 DoD 的「402 → 結算 → 取包」由 mock 變真。  
**範圍**：CDP 賬號與 API key、JWT 認證、`/verify` + `/settle` 雙端點、Agent 端 EIP-3009 簽名、base-sepolia 測試 Agent、端到端驗收。**不含** Base mainnet 上線、市集 listing 提交、真實資金。

---

## 1. 核心決策

| 問題 | 決策 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|
| verify 與 settle 的順序 | **先 `/verify` 預檢，成功後 `/settle` 上鏈，settle 成功才返貨** | settle-then-return 對首次接入最安全；Agent 拿到真實 txHash |
| 失敗時是否扣款 | `/verify` 失敗不扣；`/settle` 失敗不返貨，零資損 | x402 設計如此；用戶不會被扣款但拿不到東西 |
| 是否支援延遲結算 | **v1.4 不做** | 延遲結算（先返貨後 settle）可降延遲，但失敗時賣方損失；v2 再優化 |
| 網絡 | **base-sepolia（eip155:84532）測試，base（eip155:8453）生產** | 兩個環境共用代碼，只換環境變量 |
| Agent 端簽名 | **EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`** | x402 exact scheme 標準；USDC 原生支援 |
| Facilitator 備用 | **v1.4 只接 CDP，不做 fallback** | 多 facilitator 屬 v2；v1.4 先打通單一路徑 |
| 結算確認深度 | **1 個確認**（Base 出塊後即可） | Base L2 約 2 秒出塊；1 conf 足夠微支付 |
| 退款 | **不做** | x402 無退款語義；失敗即不返貨；v2 再議 |

**與 v1.1 的差異**：v1.1 的 `handleBuy` 在 mock 模式下只要 `txHash` 存在就返貨；v1.4 必須真實調用 CDP `/verify` + `/settle`，拿到鏈上 txHash 才寫入 `payments` 表並返貨。

---

## 2. 前置條件

### 2.1 賬號與憑證

| 項目 | 獲取方式 | 存儲位置 |
|---|---|---|
| CDP 賬號 | https://portal.cdp.coinbase.com | — |
| CDP API Key ID | Portal → API Keys → 新建 | `wrangler secret put CDP_API_KEY_ID` |
| CDP API Key Secret | 同上，創建時一次性顯示 | `wrangler secret put CDP_API_KEY_SECRET` |
| 收款錢包地址 | 自備（MetaMask / Coinbase Wallet） | `wrangler.toml` `PAY_TO` |
| base-sepolia USDC | Circle faucet 或 CDP faucet | 轉入收款錢包或 Agent 錢包 |
| Agent 測試錢包私鑰 | 自備 | 本地 `.env`，**不入 git** |

**base-sepolia faucet**：
- CDP Faucet：Portal → Faucets → Base Sepolia（每日限額）
- Circle Faucet：https://faucet.circle.com（選 Base Sepolia）

### 2.2 網絡常量

| 項目 | base-sepolia | base mainnet |
|---|---|---|
| CAIP-2 | `eip155:84532` | `eip155:8453` |
| Chain ID | 84532 | 8453 |
| USDC 合約 | `0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e` | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` |
| USDC EIP-712 name | `USDC` | `USD Coin` |
| USDC EIP-712 version | `2` | `2` |
| RPC（只讀校驗用） | `https://sepolia.base.org` | `https://mainnet.base.org` |
| Explorer | `https://sepolia.basescan.org` | `https://basescan.org` |

**注意 base-sepolia 的 EIP-712 name 是 `USDC`，不是 `USD Coin`**——這是 Circle 在測試網的已知差異，簽名域寫錯會導致 verify 失敗。生產環境用 `USD Coin`。

### 2.3 依賴

v1.1 已有 `@noble/ed25519`、`@noble/hashes`。v1.4 追加：

```json
"dependencies": {
  "viem": "^2.21.0"
}
```

`viem` 用於 Agent 端 EIP-712 簽名與 EIP-3009 授權構造；Worker 端不需要（Worker 只調 facilitator HTTP API）。

## 3. Worker 端：CDP Facilitator 接入

### 3.1 `wrangler.toml` 更新

```toml

NETWORK = "eip155:84532"                                # 測試環境
ASSET_USDC = "0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e" # base-sepolia USDC
PAY_TO = "0xREPLACE_WITH_YOUR_WALLET"
FACILITATOR_URL = "https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402"
CDP_HOST = "api.cdp.coinbase.com"
MAX_TTL_DAYS = "180"
# MOCK_FACILITATOR 移除，或設為 "0"

# 秘密（wrangler secret put）
# CDP_API_KEY_ID
# CDP_API_KEY_SECRET
```

生產切換時只改 `NETWORK`、`ASSET_USDC`，其餘不變。

### 3.2 CDP JWT 認證

CDP API 用 ES256 JWT（ECDSA P-256），不是 Ed25519。密鑰是 base64 編碼的 PKCS#8 私鑰。Web Crypto 的 ECDSA 簽名輸出是 IEEE P1363 raw 格式（r||s 共 64 字節），JWT 需要 DER 編碼，需手動轉換。

`src/worker/cdp-jwt.ts`：

```ts
// CDP JWT (ES256) 生成
// 密鑰：base64 編碼的 PKCS#8 私鑰

function base64UrlEncode(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
}

function base64UrlEncodeStr(s: string): string {
  return base64UrlEncode(new TextEncoder().encode(s));
}

// IEEE P1363 raw (r||s, 64 bytes) -> DER SEQUENCE
function rawToDer(raw: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  if (raw.length !== 64) throw new Error("expected_64_byte_signature");
  const encodeInt = (n: Uint8Array): Uint8Array => {
    let i = 0;
    while (i < n.length - 1 && n[i] === 0) i++;
    let v = n.slice(i);
    if (v[0] & 0x80) v = new Uint8Array([0, ...v]);
    return new Uint8Array([0x02, v.length, ...v]);
  };
  const r = encodeInt(raw.slice(0, 32));
  const s = encodeInt(raw.slice(32));
  return new Uint8Array([0x30, r.length + s.length, ...r, ...s]);
}

async function importPkcs8(b64: string): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), c => c.charCodeAt(0));
  return crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "pkcs8",
    bytes.buffer,
    { name: "ECDSA", namedCurve: "P-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
}

export async function generateCdpJwt(
  apiKeyId: string,
  apiKeySecretB64: string,
  method: string,
  path: string,
  host: string,
  ttlSec = 120
): Promise<string> {
  const header = {
    alg: "ES256",
    kid: apiKeyId,
    typ: "JWT",
    nonce: crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, "")
  };
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const claims = {
    sub: apiKeyId,
    iss: "cdp",
    nbf: now,
    exp: now + ttlSec,
    aud: "cdp_service",
    uris: [`${method} ${host}${path}`]
  };

  const signingInput =
    base64UrlEncodeStr(JSON.stringify(header)) + "." +
    base64UrlEncodeStr(JSON.stringify(claims));

  const key = await importPkcs8(apiKeySecretB64);
  const sigRaw = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign(
    { name: "ECDSA", hash: "SHA-256" },
    key,
    new TextEncoder().encode(signingInput)
  ));
  const sigDer = rawToDer(sigRaw);

  return signingInput + "." + base64UrlEncode(sigDer);
}
```

**關鍵點**：
- `uris` 必須精確匹配 `"METHOD host/path"`，例如 `"POST api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402/verify"`。CDP 會校驗，不匹配即 401。
- `nonce` 必須唯一（用 UUID）。
- JWT 有效期建議 120 秒，足夠一次請求。
- **每次請求重新生成 JWT**，不緩存。

### 3.3 Facilitator 調用封裝

`src/worker/facilitator.ts`：

```ts
import { generateCdpJwt } from "./cdp-jwt";

export interface PaymentRequirements {
  scheme: "exact";
  network: string;
  maxAmountRequired: string;
  resource: string;
  description: string;
  mimeType: string;
  payTo: string;
  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;
  asset: string;
  extra: { name: string; version: string };
}

export interface PaymentPayload {
  x402Version: number;
  scheme: "exact";
  network: string;
  payload: {
    signature: string;
    authorization: {
      from: string;
      to: string;
      value: string;
      validAfter: string;
      validBefore: string;
      nonce: string;
    };
  };
}

export interface VerifyResult {
  isValid: boolean;
  invalidReason?: string;
  payer?: string;
}

export interface SettleResult {
  success: boolean;
  transaction?: string;
  network?: string;
  payer?: string;
  errorReason?: string;
}

interface Env {
  CDP_API_KEY_ID: string;
  CDP_API_KEY_SECRET: string;
  CDP_HOST: string;
  FACILITATOR_URL: string;
}

async function cdpPost<T>(
  env: Env,
  path: string,
  body: unknown
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; status: number; data: T }> {
  const url = `https://${env.CDP_HOST}${path}`;
  const jwt = await generateCdpJwt(
    env.CDP_API_KEY_ID,
    env.CDP_API_KEY_SECRET,
    "POST",
    path,
    env.CDP_HOST
  );

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "authorization": `Bearer ${jwt}`,
      "content-type": "application/json"
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body)
  });

  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data: data as T };
}

export async function cdpVerify(
  env: Env,
  paymentPayload: PaymentPayload,
  requirements: PaymentRequirements
): Promise<VerifyResult> {
  const { ok, status, data } = await cdpPost<VerifyResult>(
    env,
    "/platform/v2/x402/verify",
    { paymentPayload, paymentRequirements: requirements }
  );
  if (!ok) {
    return { isValid: false, invalidReason: `http_${status}:${(data as any)?.message ?? "verify_failed"}` };
  }
  return data;
}

export async function cdpSettle(
  env: Env,
  paymentPayload: PaymentPayload,
  requirements: PaymentRequirements
): Promise<SettleResult> {
  const { ok, status, data } = await cdpPost<SettleResult>(
    env,
    "/platform/v2/x402/settle",
    { paymentPayload, paymentRequirements: requirements }
  );
  if (!ok) {
    return { success: false, errorReason: `http_${status}:${(data as any)?.message ?? "settle_failed"}` };
  }
  return data;
}
```

**校驗點**：
- `/verify` 返回 `{ isValid, invalidReason?, payer? }`
- `/settle` 返回 `{ success, transaction?, payer?, errorReason? }`
- `transaction` 是鏈上 txHash；`payer` 是付款地址
- HTTP 4xx/5xx 都要當作失敗處理

### 3.4 更新 `handleBuy`

`src/worker/buy.ts` 重寫：

```ts
import { json, Env } from "./index";
import { derivePseudonym } from "./pseudonym";
import { cdpVerify, cdpSettle, PaymentPayload, PaymentRequirements } from "./facilitator";

export async function handleBuy(req: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const id = new URL(req.url).pathname.split("/").pop()!;
  const status = await env.KV.get(`status:${id}`);
  if (status === "recalled") {
    const replacement = await env.KV.get(`replacement:${id}`);
    return json({ error: "pack_recalled", replacement_package_id: replacement ?? null }, 410);
  }

  const paymentHeader = req.headers.get("x-payment");
  if (!paymentHeader) return paymentRequired(id, env);

  let payment: PaymentPayload;
  try {
    payment = JSON.parse(atob(paymentHeader));
  } catch {
    return json({ error: "bad_payment_header" }, 400);
  }

  if (payment.scheme !== "exact" || payment.network !== env.NETWORK) {
    return json({ error: "unsupported_scheme", expected: { scheme: "exact", network: env.NETWORK } }, 403);
  }

  const requirements = buildRequirements(id, env);

  // 1. /verify 預檢
  const verify = await cdpVerify(env, payment, requirements);
  if (!verify.isValid) {
    return json({ error: "payment_invalid", reason: verify.invalidReason }, 402);
  }

  // 2. /settle 上鏈
  const settle = await cdpSettle(env, payment, requirements);
  if (!settle.success || !settle.transaction) {
    return json({ error: "settlement_failed", reason: settle.errorReason }, 402);
  }

  const txHash = settle.transaction;
  const payer = settle.payer ?? verify.payer ?? "unknown";

  // 3. 防重放（settle 成功後才寫，避免重複 settle）
  const seen = await env.KV.get(`tx:${txHash}`);
  if (seen) {
    // 已結算過，直接返貨（idempotent）
    return deliverPack(id, env, txHash, payer);
  }
  await env.KV.put(`tx:${txHash}`, "1", { expirationTtl: 86400 * 30 });

  // 4. 記錄
  const pseudonym = derivePseudonym(payer);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    "INSERT INTO payments (agent_pseudonym, package_id, amount_usdc, tx_hash, called_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)"
  ).bind(pseudonym, id, requirements.maxAmountRequired, txHash, new Date().toISOString()).run();

  return deliverPack(id, env, txHash, payer);
}

async function deliverPack(id: string, env: Env, txHash: string, payer: string) {
  const pkg = await env.KV.get(`pkg:${id}`, "json");
  const v = await env.KV.get(`verify:${id}`, "json");
  if (!pkg) return json({ error: "package_not_found" }, 404);

  return json({
    data: (pkg as any)?.data ?? pkg,
    verification_snapshot: v ? { ...(v as any), snapshotted_at: new Date().toISOString() } : null,
    receipt: {
      tx_hash: txHash,
      network: env.NETWORK,
      amount_usdc: (v as any)?.price_usdc ?? "5000",
      payer_pseudonym: derivePseudonym(payer),
      explorer: explorerUrl(env.NETWORK, txHash)
    }
  });
}

function explorerUrl(network: string, txHash: string): string {
  const base = network === "eip155:8453"
    ? "https://basescan.org"
    : "https://sepolia.basescan.org";
  return `${base}/tx/${txHash}`;
}

export function buildRequirements(id: string, env: Env): PaymentRequirements {
  return {
    scheme: "exact",
    network: env.NETWORK,
    maxAmountRequired: "5000",   // 0.005 USDC（6 位小數）
    resource: `${env.OWNSTIE_BASE}/buy/${id}`,
    description: `Verified data package ${id}`,
    mimeType: "application/json",
    payTo: env.PAY_TO,
    maxTimeoutSeconds: 300,
    asset: env.ASSET_USDC,
    extra: {
      name: env.NETWORK === "eip155:8453" ? "USD Coin" : "USDC",
      version: "2"
    }
  };
}

function paymentRequired(id: string, env: Env) {
  return json({
    x402Version: 1,
    accepts: [buildRequirements(id, env)]
  }, 402);
}
```

**關鍵改動**：
1. `MOCK_FACILITATOR` 分支移除，一律走真實 facilitator
2. `/verify` 失敗返回 402（不是 403），因為 402 語義是「payment required」，Agent 可換一個新的支付 payload 重試
3. `/settle` 成功後才寫 `tx:` 防重放 key
4. 冪等：同一 txHash 重複請求直接返貨，不重複 settle
5. `receipt.explorer` 給 Agent 可點擊的區塊瀏覽器鏈接

### 3.5 `Env` 類型更新

`src/worker/index.ts`：

```ts
export interface Env {
  KV: KVNamespace;
  DB: D1Database;
  OWNSTIE_BASE: string;
  NETWORK: string;
  ASSET_USDC: string;
  PAY_TO: string;
  FACILITATOR_URL: string;
  CDP_HOST: string;
  CDP_API_KEY_ID: string;
  CDP_API_KEY_SECRET: string;
  SYNC_SECRET?: string;
  ALERT_WEBHOOK?: string;
}
```

---

## 4. Agent 端：EIP-3009 簽名

### 4.1 為甚麼是 EIP-3009

x402 exact scheme 用 USDC 的 `transferWithAuthorization`（EIP-3009），不是普通 `transfer`。原因是：
- Agent 簽名授權，不需即時上鏈
- 賣方（或 facilitator）代為提交，Agent 不需付 gas
- `validAfter` / `validBefore` 控制有效期
- `nonce` 防重放（鏈上合約保證）

EIP-712 簽名域（base-sepolia）：

```ts
{
  name: "USDC",           // mainnet 是 "USD Coin"
  version: "2",
  chainId: 84532,          // 8453 for mainnet
  verifyingContract: "0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e"
}
```

消息類型：

```ts
const TRANSFER_WITH_AUTHORIZATION_TYPES = {
  TransferWithAuthorization: [
    { name: "from", type: "address" },
    { name: "to", type: "address" },
    { name: "value", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "validAfter", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "validBefore", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "nonce", type: "bytes32" }
  ]
};
```

### 4.2 Agent 支付構造

`scripts/agent-pay.ts`：

```ts
import {
  createWalletClient, createPublicClient, http,
  encodeFunctionData, parseAbi, keccak256, toBytes, pad
} from "viem";
import { privateKeyToAccount } from "viem/accounts";
import { baseSepolia } from "viem/chains";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

export interface AgentPayInput {
  rpcUrl: string;
  privateKey: `0x${string}`;
  chainId: 84532 | 8453;
  usdcAddress: `0x${string}`;
  usdcName: string;        // "USDC" for sepolia, "USD Coin" for mainnet
  payTo: `0x${string}`;
  amount: bigint;           // in smallest unit (6 decimals for USDC)
  resourceUrl: string;
  network: string;          // "eip155:84532" or "eip155:8453"
}

const TRANSFER_WITH_AUTHORIZATION_TYPES = {
  TransferWithAuthorization: [
    { name: "from", type: "address" },
    { name: "to", type: "address" },
    { name: "value", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "validAfter", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "validBefore", type: "uint256" },
    { name: "nonce", type: "bytes32" }
  ]
} as const;

export async function buildPaymentHeader(input: AgentPayInput): Promise<string> {
  const account = privateKeyToAccount(input.privateKey);
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

  const domain = {
    name: input.usdcName,
    version: "2",
    chainId: input.chainId,
    verifyingContract: input.usdcAddress
  } as const;

  const message = {
    from: account.address,
    to: input.payTo,
    value: input.amount,
    validAfter: 0n,
    validBefore: BigInt(now + 300),  // 5 分鐘有效
    nonce: ("0x" + randomBytes(32).toString("hex")) as `0x${string}`
  } as const;

  const signature = await account.signTypedData({
    domain,
    types: TRANSFER_WITH_AUTHORIZATION_TYPES,
    primaryType: "TransferWithAuthorization",
    message
  });

  const paymentPayload = {
    x402Version: 1,
    scheme: "exact",
    network: input.network,
    payload: {
      signature,
      authorization: {
        from: message.from,
        to: message.to,
        value: message.value.toString(),
        validAfter: message.validAfter.toString(),
        validBefore: message.validBefore.toString(),
        nonce: message.nonce
      }
    }
  };

  return btoa(JSON.stringify(paymentPayload));
}
```

**關鍵點**：
- `value` 用 string（bigint 轉 string），避免 JSON 精度丟失
- `validBefore = now + 300`，5 分鐘有效；太長會被 facilitator 拒，太短會超時
- `nonce` 用 32 字節隨機數，**不能重用**（鏈上合約會拒）
- 簽名域 `name` 在 sepolia 是 `"USDC"`，mainnet 是 `"USD Coin"`，**寫錯即 verify 失敗**

### 4.3 更新測試 Agent

`scripts/test-agent.ts` 第 8 步替換為真實支付：

```ts
import { buildPaymentHeader } from "./agent-pay";

// 7. 402 challenge
const challenge = await fetch(`${BASE}/buy/${pkg.package_id}`, { headers: { "x-agent-id": AGENT_ID } });
if (challenge.status !== 402) throw new Error("expected 402");
const accept = (await challenge.json()).accepts[0];

// 8. 真實支付
const agentPrivKey = process.env.AGENT_PRIVATE_KEY as `0x${string}`;
if (!agentPrivKey) throw new Error("AGENT_PRIVATE_KEY required");

const paymentHeader = await buildPaymentHeader({
  rpcUrl: process.env.RPC_URL ?? "https://sepolia.base.org",
  privateKey: agentPrivKey,
  chainId: 84532,
  usdcAddress: "0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e",
  usdcName: "USDC",
  payTo: accept.payTo,
  amount: BigInt(accept.maxAmountRequired),
  resourceUrl: accept.resource,
  network: accept.network
});

const bought = await fetch(`${BASE}/buy/${pkg.package_id}`, {
  headers: { "x-payment": paymentHeader, "x-agent-id": AGENT_ID }
});
if (!bought.ok) {
  const err = await bought.text();
  throw new Error(`buy failed: ${bought.status} ${err}`);
}
const pack = await bought.json();
console.log("purchase OK, tx:", pack.receipt.tx_hash);
console.log("explorer:", pack.receipt.explorer);

// 9. 鏈上確認：用 RPC 查 tx receipt
const receipt = await fetch(process.env.RPC_URL ?? "https://sepolia.base.org", {
  method: "POST",
  headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({
    jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method: "eth_getTransactionReceipt",
    params: [pack.receipt.tx_hash]
  })
}).then(r => r.json());
if (!receipt.result || receipt.result.status !== "0x1") {
  throw new Error("on_chain_tx_not_confirmed");
}
console.log("on-chain confirmed, block:", receipt.result.blockNumber);
```

**驗收點**：txHash 必須在 base-sepolia 上真實存在且 status=1。

---

## 5. 端到端測試流程

### 5.1 環境變量

`.env.local`（不入 git）：

```bash
AGENT_PRIVATE_KEY=0x...                    # 測試錢包私鑰
RPC_URL=https://sepolia.base.org
BASE=https://ownsite.dev                   # 或 http://localhost:8787
```

`wrangler.toml` 已配 `NETWORK=eip155:84532`、`ASSET_USDC=base-sepolia USDC`、`CDP_HOST`、`FACILITATOR_URL`。

`wrangler secret` 已配 `CDP_API_KEY_ID`、`CDP_API_KEY_SECRET`。

### 5.2 運行順序

```bash
# 1. 本地 dev（可選，Worker 調 CDP 仍走真實 facilitator）
npm run dev

# 2. 另一終端跑測試 Agent
BASE=http://localhost:8787 AGENT_PRIVATE_KEY=0x... npm run test-agent

# 3. 觀察輸出
# signature OK
# evidence hash OK: https://example.org/report/2026-09 para:4-7
# 402 challenge OK, amount=5000
# purchase OK, tx: 0x...
# explorer: https://sepolia.basescan.org/tx/0x...
# on-chain confirmed, block: 12345678
# replay blocked OK
# status: fresh
```

### 5.3 失敗排查

| 症狀 | 可能原因 | 排查 |
|---|---|---|
| `/verify` 返回 `invalidReason: "insufficient_funds"` | Agent 錢包 USDC 不足 | 查 base-sepolia USDC 餘額 |
| `invalidReason: "invalid_signature"` | 簽名域 `name` 錯（sepolia 用 `USDC`）或 `chainId` 錯 | 檢查 `usdcName` 與 `chainId` |
| `invalidReason: "authorization_expired"` | `validBefore` 已過 | 重新簽名，延長有效期 |
| `invalidReason: "nonce_already_used"` | nonce 重用 | 每次支付生成新 nonce |
| `/settle` 返回 `errorReason: "insufficient_gas"` | facilitator 錢包 gas 不足（不是你的問題） | 聯繫 CDP support |
| Worker 返回 401 from CDP | JWT `uris` 不匹配或 `kid` 錯 | 檢查 `generateCdpJwt` 的 path 參數 |
| Worker 返回 500 | facilitator 超時 | 重試；檢查 CDP status page |
| `txHash` 在 explorer 找不到 | settle 返回但鏈上未確認 | 等 30 秒重查；若仍無，聯繫 CDP |

**最常見錯誤**：EIP-712 域的 `name`。sepolia 是 `"USDC"`，mainnet 是 `"USD Coin"`。寫錯時 `/verify` 返回 `invalid_signature`，但錯誤信息不會告訴你是域錯，需自己比對。

---

## 6. 測試用例與驗收

### 6.1 Phase 1 DoD 追加（v1.4）

| 用例 | 期望 | 通過標準 |
|---|---|---|
| 402 challenge | 返回 402 + `accepts[0].network === "eip155:84532"` | ✓ |
| 真實支付 | `/verify` + `/settle` 成功，返回 `txHash` | ✓ |
| 鏈上確認 | `eth_getTransactionReceipt` status=`0x1` | ✓ |
| 餘額變動 | Agent 錢包 USDC 減少 `maxAmountRequired` | ✓ |
| 收款到賬 | `PAY_TO` 錢包 USDC 增加 `maxAmountRequired`（扣 facilitator fee 後） | ✓ |
| 重放拒絕 | 同一 `x-payment` 重打返回已結算包（冪等）或 `tx_replay` | ✓ |
| 簽名域錯誤 | 用 `USD Coin` 在 sepolia 簽名 → `/verify` 返回 `invalid_signature` | ✓ |
| 餘額不足 | 空錢包支付 → `/verify` 返回 `insufficient_funds` | ✓ |
| 過期授權 | `validBefore` 設為過去 → `/verify` 返回 `authorization_expired` | ✓ |
| 損壞 JWT | 改 CDP secret → `/verify` 返回 401 | ✓ |

### 6.2 手動驗收清單

- [ ] base-sepolia 瀏覽器能搜到 txHash
- [ ] `receipt.explorer` 鏈接可點開
- [ ] `payments` 表有記錄，`tx_hash` 與 receipt 一致
- [ ] `agent_pseudonym` 是 SHA-256 派生，不是原始地址
- [ ] `/settle` 失敗時 `payments` 表無記錄（不扣款不返貨）
- [ ] 同一 `txHash` 重複請求不重複寫 `payments`

---

## 7. 遷移到 Base Mainnet

**只改環境變量，不改代碼**：

```toml
NETWORK = "eip155:8453"
ASSET_USDC = "0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913"
```

Agent 端：
- `chainId: 8453`
- `usdcName: "USD Coin"`
- `usdcAddress: "0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913"`

`PAY_TO` 必須是主網錢包（可與測試網相同地址，但 USDC 是主網的）。

**上線前額外檢查**：
- [ ] `PAY_TO` 私鑰安全保管（建議硬體錢包或多簽）
- [ ] CDP API key 有 production 權限
- [ ] 收費金額單位正確（USDC 6 位小數；$0.50 = `500000`）
- [ ] `/settle` 的 facilitator fee 已計入定價（CDP 可能收手續費，需查最新費率）
- [ ] 監控告警已接（settle 失敗率 > 5%）

---

## 8. 風險與局限

| 風險 | 說明 | 對策 |
|---|---|---|
| **CDP 單點依賴** | CDP facilitator 掛則無法結算 | v1.4 接受；v2 加 fallback facilitator（如 public x402 facilitator） |
| **JWT 時鐘偏移** | Worker 與 CDP 時鐘差 > 120s 會 401 | Cloudflare 時鐘可靠；本地 dev 用 `wrangler dev` 同步 |
| **Settle 延遲** | Base L2 約 2 秒，但 facilitator 可能排隊 | 設 30 秒超時；超時返回 504 並提示 Agent 重試（用新 nonce） |
| **Facilitator fee** | CDP 可能收 settle 手續費 | 查 CDP 最新費率；定價時計入 |
| **Nonce 碰撞** | 32 字節隨機碰撞概率極低 | 接受 |
| **錢包私鑰洩露** | Agent 測試錢包私鑰若洩露，資金被盜 | 只用測試網小額；生產用硬體錢包或 KMS |
| **`validBefore` 太短** | 簽名後 5 分鐘未提交即過期 | 5 分鐘足夠；若網絡慢可延至 10 分鐘 |
| **USDC 合約升級** | Circle 可能升級合約 | 監控 Circle 公告；合約地址寫入配置而非硬編碼 |

**最關鍵局限**：v1.4 只驗證「能完成一次真實結算」，**未驗證高並發、長時間運行、facilitator 故障恢復**。這些屬 Phase 2 壓測與監控。

---

| 文件 | 狀態 |
|---|---|
| `src/worker/cdp-jwt.ts` | 新增 |
| `src/worker/facilitator.ts` | 新增 |

**Phase 1 DoD 最終狀態**（v1.4 完成後）：
- ✅ 測試 Agent 無人類干預完成發現→驗簽→refetch→preview→402→真實結算→取包→TTL
- ✅ 簽名離線驗證成功（v1.1）
- ✅ evidence refetch hash 比對成功（v1.2）
- ✅ Pages→KV 同步健康（v1.3）
- ✅ base-sepolia 真實 USDC 結算，txHash 鏈上可查（v1.4）
- ✅ 重放拒絕、冪等返貨

**Phase 1 正式結束，可進入 Phase 2**（擴充分發）。
